Rather than optimizing for conventional performance metrics, we quantify structured behavioral displacement using dialect marker frequency, lexical proximity to standardized Arabic, and structural variance. Lightweight stabilization nearly doubles dialect marker usage, increasing it from 0.2266 to 0.4988, while significantly reducing structural instability. Pivot mediation introduces normalization pressure and measurable compression effects, whereas zero-shot translation exhibits the highest decision variance. We argue that translation divergence across agents reveals latent decision flexibility within multilingual models and we provide a principled interpretability framework for low-resource dialect generation.

</details>


### [172] [Sequential Beats Joint: On the Interplay between On-Policy Distillation and RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04108)
*Boyan Li,Bingsen Chen,Chenghao Yang,Ping Nie,Chen Zhao,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单的两阶段训练方案"OPD-then-RL"（先进行同策略蒸馏，再进行基于可验证奖励的强化学习），在逻辑与数学推理基准上一致优于纯OPD、纯RLVR以及所有联合优化基线。作者通过pass@k、学习动力学与参数更新等系统性分析给出了统一的解释：OPD负责扩大学生对教师支持解的覆盖范围，RL在此支持范围内进一步锐化；而联合优化两个信号会导致相互干扰。此外，OPD验证分数是切换至RL阶段的关键信号，且OPD比SFT更适合作为RL的冷启动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR与OPD是推理大模型后训练的两大主流方法。先前的混合方法试图在单步内将两者的信号融合（加权相加或教师调制缩放RL优势），但作者质疑这种"纠缠式"融合是否真的最优。本文的核心动机是探索能否通过更简单的两阶段序列化方式，将OPD的密集令牌级监督与RL的稀疏奖励互补优势更好地发挥出来，并揭示其背后的机制。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线：第一阶段使用OPD（同策略蒸馏）进行训练，第二阶段切换到RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）继续训练。作者通过实验对比纯OPD、纯RLVR、联合优化基线（加权相加、教师调制重缩放）与两阶段方案，并从pass@k行为、学习动力学和参数更新三个维度进行机制分析，还探索了阶段切换时机（以OPD验证分数为信号）与冷启动方式（OPD vs SFT）的实际配方。

Result: 实验结果表明，两阶段"OPD-then-RL"方案在逻辑和数学推理基准上均一致优于纯OPD、纯RLVR以及所有联合（融合式）基线。机制分析将原因归结为：OPD与RL分别扮演"扩大覆盖"与"支持内锐化"的角色，而联合优化时两个信号会产生干扰。此外，OPD验证分数可作为切换到RL的可靠信号，OPD比SFT作为RL冷启动更优。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将OPD与RL由"纠缠信号"转为"互补阶段"是一个简单而强力的组合策略。两阶段方案不仅带来一致的性能提升，还提供了清晰的机制解释（先覆盖后锐化）与实用的工程配方（以OPD验证分数决定切换时机、以OPD代替SFT作为冷启动），为推理LLM的后训练提供了新的方法论参照。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have emerged as two dominant methods for post-training reasoning LLMs. Prior work uses OPD's dense token-level supervision to complement the sparse RL reward, fusing the two signals within a single step: either as a \emph{weighted-additive combination} or a \emph{teacher-modulated rescaling} of the RL advantage. In this paper, we show that a simple two-stage scheme, OPD-then-RL, consistently outperforms pure OPD, pure RLVR, and all such joint baselines across logic and math reasoning benchmarks. Beyond the empirical results, we further provide a systematic understanding of this through pass@$k$ behavior, learning dynamics, and parameter updates, yielding a consistent explanation: OPD expands the student's coverage of teacher-supported solutions and RL sharpens within that support, while jointly optimizing the two signals causes them to interfere.To provide a practical recipe, we find that the OPD validation score is the key signal for when to switch to RL, and that OPD is a better cold start for RL than SFT. Together, our results establish OPD-then-RL as a simple yet strong way to combine the two methods, turning two entangled signals into complementary stages.

</details>


### [173] [Last Translation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04173)
*Vilém Zouhar,Niyati Bafna,Mukund Choudhary,Maike Züfle,Sara Rajaee,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Sara Papi,Ona de Gibert,Bhavitvya Malik,Eliya Habba,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Patrícia Schmidtová,Michelle Wastl,Sheriff Issaka,Leshem Choshen,Stella Biderman,Antonis Anastasopoulos,Jan Niehues,Rico Sennrich,Mrinmaya Sachan,Ondřej Bojar,Kenton Murray,Jörg Tiedemann,Alham Fikri Aji,Philipp Koehn,Christof Monz,Alexandra Birch,Sowmya Vajjala,Chalamalasetti Kranti,Cristina España-Bonet,Nobin Sarwar,David Kaczér,Shunta Asano,Malik Marmonier,Daban Q. Jaff,Vaisakhi Mishra,Hend Al- Khalifa,Gabriele Sarti,Sourajit Saha,Nils Rehlinger,Juan Daniel Cuervo Villa,Jonathan Tonglet,Saugata Purkayastha,Dominik Macháček,Jagannathan Ramanujam,Heejin Do,Zuzana Nadova,Fred Philippy,Fabian Retkowski,Maria Lymperaiou,Silvia Casola,Hanna Yukhymenko,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Sangwon Ryu,Andrés Jerez,Ron Keinan,Shuaib Shuaib Yusuf,Avantica Vempati,Maria Carmen Staiano,Sukannya Purkayastha,Adrian Cosma,Vitalii Babenko,Erivan Inan,Aviral Nigam,Wafa Aissa,Fatima Haouari,Venkata Prasanth Kumar Gummadi,Mehdi Jafarzadeh,Valentin Scourneau,Lukas Edman,Kaiser Sun,Shaomu Tan,Mohammad Sadegh Gholizadeh,Johannes-Rudolf David,Dipankar Srirag,Javier García Gilabert,Ruta Binkyte,Manar Ali,Ana-Maria Bucur,Sabry E. Farrag,Youssef Saber,Yihong Liu,Jean Maillard,Cojocaru Nicoleta,Xiaochuang Yuan,Sina Ahmadi,Philipp Mondorf,Kaustubh Dhole,Roman Wixinger,Shenbin Qian,Manuel Tuor,Sergey Troshin,Jonathan Yahav,Fida Mohammad Thoker,Amir Arsalan Rezapour,Lance Calvin Lim Gamboa,Manon Reusens,Kätriin Kukk,Koel Dutta Chowdhury,Giuseppe Gallipoli,Christian Hoang,Shaswati Saha,Seth Aycock,Jan Kocoń,Bo Chen,Linh Vu,Vatsal Venkatkrishna,Arafat Ahsan,Luan Thanh Nguyen,Hassan Soliman,Daryna Dementieva,Theresia Veronika Rampisela,Ngoc Quynh Tram Do,Marius Huber,Kazuki Egashira,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Vladislav Poritski,Mike Zhang,Deep Shah,Paul Gavrikov,Luis Frentzen Salim,David Africa,R. Damanhuri,Bello Umar Bello,Anumit Garg,Gengyu Rao,Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi,Kamile Dementaviciute,Andrianos Michail,L D M S Sai Teja,Dawei Zhu,Yi Fan,Wei Liu,Farhan Farsi,Elias Herranen,Sankalan Pal Chowdhury,Karen Sanchez,Farzad Shami,Ashok Urlana,Zimu Wang,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Priyaranjan Pattnayak,Marii Ojastu,Hongbin Na,Emilian Radoi,Chenyi Zhao,Carlos Hinojosa,Andrea Gregor de Varda,Zaid Alyafeai,Reem Alzahrani,Nehal Kathrotia,Alex Flückiger,Ulysses Sekai Tully Carr,Jimson Paulo Layacan,Guy Kaplan,Ritwik Tiwari,Rishit Dagli,Oksana Volchek,Isaac R Caswell,Bowen Yi,Blanka Kövér,Amir Hossein Yari,Aicha Chorana,Zhengxiang Wang,Selja Keränen,Samuel Simko,Joy Olusanya,Jenny Chim,Enzo Doyen,Vivek Harsha Lakkamaneni,Sophia Conrad,Pouya Sadeghi,Panayiotis Panayiotou,Luis Lara,Jannatul Nayem,Eran Yahav,Debanshu Das,Antonia Karamolegkou,Anmol Goel,Aishik Mandal,Tommaso Cerruti,Raoyuan Zhao,Mykola Haltiuk,Thura Aung,Naser Almousa,Amir Hossein Kargaran,Rachel Bawden,Qiaoyuan Zheng,Mateusz Lango,Beni Egressy,Fidel Rodríguez Velásquez,Natchapon Jongwiriyanurak,Minh Ngoc Do,Marco Gaido,Lena Libon,Dzmitry Kuzmin,Badal Nyalang,Antoine Taroni,Andrei Niculae,Abdulaziz Nura Kani,Rushikesh Zawar,Marek Šuppa,Beatrice Savoldi,Andreas Simons,Rayyan Merchant,Ilai Yaron Levy,Francesco Pinto,Ziyi Yang,Yolanda Xavier,Samuel Frontull,Muhammad Ravi Shulthan Habibi,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Harris Abdul Majid,Francesca Padovani,Tim Graf,Tatiana Bielakova,Sharifa Djurabaeva,Shaoxiong Ji,Raia Abu Ahmad,Pavel Stepachev,Jirui Qi,Ayush Sunil Munot,Alireza Pakniat,Ayla Rigouts Terryn,Yuxing Lu,Yurii Paniv,Xiyan Fu,Tosin Adewumi,Sunisth Kumar,Stéphane J. P. S. Thunus,Shree Harsha Bokkahalli Satish,Shayan Bali,Prakhar Gupta,Papa Abdou Karim Karou Diallo,Matija Akrap,Marko Culjak,Kristýna Onderková,Joseph Attieh,Esrael Teferi Tensay,Elisabeth Fittschen,Benoît Sagot,Jingwei Ni,Yu Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了"最后翻译基准"(Last Translation Benchmark, LTB)，这是一个由人工撰写并经同行评审的活数据集，包含文本、图像、音频和视频等多种模态的示例，旨在击破当前最先进的机器翻译模型。该基准采用手工制定的验证规则来描述每个示例上的具体失败案例，提供可靠且可操作的评价，并支持持续贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型能力增强，标准机器翻译基准已接近饱和；自动翻译指标不可靠、易被奖励黑客攻击且缺乏可操作性；人工黄金评价也存在可复现性、客观性和可扩展性问题。这些因素阻碍了客观追踪翻译领域的进展并识别改进路径。

Method: 构建一个活数据集(LTBv1)，收录人类作者撰写并经同行评审的多模态示例（文本、图像、音频、视频），每个示例附带手工制作的验证规则，描述在该示例上具体可验证的失败案例，使未来评估既可靠又可操作。

Result: 发布了LTBv1版本，包含2026年9月1日前接受的贡献，并计划随着新数据持续收集而进行后续版本发布。

Conclusion: LTB作为一种持续演进的基准，通过人类专家驱动的对抗性示例和精细的验证规则，为超越静态基准饱和问题提供了新路径，使翻译评估更具可靠性、可操作性和可扩展性。

Abstract: For scientific progress, we need benchmarks that test the limits of state-of-the-art models, and evaluation methods that inform us about failure cases. As models get stronger, standard benchmarks for machine translation are approaching saturation. Further, automatic translation metrics are unreliable, vulnerable to reward-hacking, and provide unactionable assessments. Even gold human evaluation is not problem-free, because it often lacks reproducibility, objectivity, and scalability. Overall, this prevents us from tracking objective progress in the field and identifying pathways for improvement. We introduce the Last Translation Benchmark, a collection of human-authored and peer-reviewed examples (texts, images, audio, videos) that break leading machine translation models. We also present a new evaluation approach: each example comes with handcrafted verification rules describing concrete failure cases on that example, therefore allowing reliable and actionable future evaluation. The Last Translation Benchmark is a live dataset that accepts ongoing contributions. The latest version is LTBv1, containing accepted contributions prior to September 1st 2026, with future releases planned as new data is continuously collected.

</details>


### [174] [Knowledge Acquisition During Pre-training? Large Language Models Learn Better With Auxiliary Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04180)
*Joseph Lee,Yidi Huang,Dokyoon Kim,Shu Yang,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统研究了大型语言模型（LLM）预训练中的知识获取机制，发现"辅助视图"（知识的重构形式）对学习具有因果帮助作用。具体而言，重复是知识习得的必要条件，但在固定token预算下，将文档重复的token分配给辅助视图反而能提升学习效果（即使对事实回忆任务也如此）。辅助视图的有效性不依赖于生成它们的教师模型强度，且存在"上下文知识"和"基础性知识"两类有助于填补先验知识缺口的知识形式。机制分析通过层间偏置和压缩揭示了这些效应的运作方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在预训练中表现出强大的知识获取能力，但我们对这一过程的内在机制理解仍然存在明显空白。作者提出"辅助视图"假说，认为知识的多样化呈现形式（重构）对学习具有因果促进作用，并通过受控实验来验证和厘清这一机制。

Method: 采用受控实验方法进行系统性验证：（1）确认重复对知识习得的必要性，并区分复述在不同batch size下的作用；（2）在固定token预算下，对比将token分配给文档重复与辅助视图的效果差异；（3）变化教师模型强度以检验辅助视图有效性的条件；（4）区分并考察两类知识（上下文知识与基础性知识）在先验知识缺口中的作用；（5）通过层间偏置和压缩指标进行机制层面的分析。

Result: （1）重复是知识习得的必要条件，但复述仅在较小batch size下才有所帮助；（2）固定token预算时，从文档重复转移token给辅助视图能提升学习效果，即使对事实回忆而言也如此（反直觉发现）；（3）辅助视图的效果不依赖教师模型的强度；（4）识别出能填补先验知识缺口的"上下文知识"和"基础性知识"两类知识形式；（5）机制分析揭示了层间偏置和压缩的变化。

Conclusion: 辅助表示的知识形式（在大型预训练语料中自然出现）是预训练成功的关键因素。这一发现为"数据多样性为何重要"提供了合理的解释，深化了我们对LLM预训练中知识获取机制的理论理解。

Abstract: Gaps remain in our understanding of how large language models (LLMs) acquire knowledge during pre-training. We posit that auxiliary views, reformulations of knowledge, are causally helpful for learning. We design controlled experiments to isolate this. First, we confirm that repetition is necessary for acquisition and clarify that paraphrasing helps only at smaller batch sizes. Second, holding the token budget fixed, allocating tokens from document repetition to auxiliary views improves learning, counterintuitively, even for factual recall. Third, the effectiveness of auxiliary views is not contingent on the strength of the teacher model that generates them. Fourth, we identify forms of knowledge, contextual and foundational, that aid learning in the presence of prior knowledge gaps. Finally, we examine how these effects manifest mechanistically via layer-wise biases and compression. Together, our findings suggest that auxiliary representations of knowledge, which arise naturally in large pre-training corpora, are a key factor in the success of pre-training and offer a plausible explanation for why data diversity matters.

</details>


### [175] [Legibility is Not Interpretability: Comparing Judged and Actual Importance in Chain-Of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04194)
*Kevin Du,Alexander Hoyle,Laura Ruis,Acyr Locatelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了思维链推理文本是否真正编码了推理步骤重要性的信息。研究者将步骤重要性量化为优势值（通过蒙特卡洛掷出估计），并评估LLM裁判能否识别高优势步骤。结果发现：虽然足够强大的LLM能超越流行率基线，但远未达到噪声上限；微调逐步评论器能显著改进错误回答的识别，但正确回答仍与上限有差距。这表明步骤重要性仅能从推理轨迹文本中部分恢复，对过程奖励建模有重要警示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量现有工作将思维链推理轨迹视为解释模型的窗口，用LLM裁判来诊断错误、评估忠实性，并基于步骤文本训练过程奖励模型和生成式评论器。这些实践隐含假设推理步骤的文本携带了其功能角色的信息。然而，文本是否真的编码了哪些推理步骤重要的信息这一问题尚待验证。

Method: 作者将推理步骤的重要性操作化为优势值（advantage），即包含该步骤对期望奖励（如产生正确答案的概率）的改变量，通过蒙特卡洛掷出进行估计。以这些估计为地面真值，评估LLM裁判能否识别高优势步骤，并训练逐步评论器模型来测试步骤重要性从文本中可恢复性的上限。

Result: LLM裁判在高优势步骤识别上能超越流行率基线，但远低于噪声上限。微调评论器对错误回答有显著改进，但正确回答仍远未达到上限，表明步骤重要性仅能部分从推理文本中恢复。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹的文本可读性不应被当作可解释性。步骤重要性信息仅部分编码在推理步骤文本中，这对过程奖励建模以及依赖思维链文本进行推理诊断的实践提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Reasoning traces from chain-of-thought models appear to offer a legible window into how a model arrives at its answer. A growing body of work treats them as such, using LLM judges to diagnose errors, evaluate faithfulness, and provide step-level supervision via process reward models and generative critics. These practices rely on the text of a reasoning step carrying information about its functional role. But does the text actually encode information about which reasoning steps matter? We operationalize the importance of a reasoning step as its advantage: the change in expected reward, e.g., producing the correct final answer, from including that step, estimated via Monte Carlo rollouts. Basing ground truth on these estimates, we evaluate whether LLM judges can identify high-advantage steps and find that sufficiently capable LLMs can outperform a prevalence baseline but fall well short of a noise ceiling. Fine-tuning a model as a step-level critic yields strong improvement for incorrect responses but remains distant from ceiling for correct responses, suggesting that step importance is only partially recoverable from the text of the reasoning trace. Our findings contribute to a growing body of chain-of-thought faithfulness work that cautions against treating the legibility of reasoning traces as interpretability, especially with implications for process reward modeling.

</details>


### [176] [ESPO: Error-Structured Prompt Optimization via Diagnose, Diversify, and Stabilize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04197)
*Lihao Liu,Peng Tang,Kunwar Yashraj Singh,Shabnam Ghadar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出ESPO（Error-Structured Prompt Optimization），针对进化式提示词优化器（如GEPA）的提示词膨胀问题，将优化分为诊断（结构化聚类训练错误）、生成（四种互补策略）和选择（bootstrap稳定性选择）三个阶段，在七个NLP基准上平均准确率提升+3.76个百分点，提示词缩短47%，且在多个跨模型实验中均取得最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化式提示词优化器（如GEPA）存在严重的提示词膨胀问题：每轮迭代不断追加规则和注意事项，导致提示词长度增长至3倍但准确率并未提升。作者将其归因于三个缺陷：错误观察不完整、搜索多样性有限、选择机制不可靠，因此需要一种更结构化的提示词优化方法。

Method: ESPO将提示词优化分解为三个阶段：(1) Diagnose：将训练错误一次性聚类为结构化错误模式；(2) Propose：通过四种具有独立偏见的互补策略生成候选提示词；(3) Select：应用bootstrap稳定性选择机制。该方法从错误结构出发系统性地优化提示词，而非简单迭代追加规则。

Result: 在Tweet、MMLU、GSM8K、HotpotQA、ScoNe、HoVer和PUPA七个基准上，ESPO平均准确率74.67%对比GEPA的70.91%，提升+3.76个百分点，每个数据集均达到或超过GEPA；提示词长度从1,878字符缩短至1,004字符（缩短47%），推理速度更快。跨四种学生模型实验（Gemma 3 12B、Mistral 14B、Qwen3 32B、Claude Haiku 4.5）中ESPO在每个模型上均取得最佳平均准确率，最大差距出现在Qwen3 GSM8K（15.00%提升至91.40%）。

Conclusion: ESPO通过错误结构化分析有效解决了提示词膨胀问题，同时提升了准确率和效率。泛化界从理论上解释了每个阶段对应测试时间差距的相应项。消融实验验证了关键预测：仅增加多样性而不加bootstrap选择反而损害性能（-1.20%），表明三个阶段缺一不可、相互配合。

Abstract: Evolutionary prompt optimizers such as GEPA suffer from prompt bloat: each iteration appends rules and caveats, producing prompts up to 3$\times$ longer yet no more accurate. We trace this to three deficiencies - incomplete error observation, limited search diversity, and unreliable selection - and propose ESPO (Error-Structured Prompt Optimization), which decomposes prompt optimization into three phases: Diagnose clusters all training errors into structural patterns in one round; Propose generates candidates via four complementary strategies with independent biases; Select applies bootstrap stability selection. On seven public NLP benchmarks - Tweet, MMLU, GSM8K, HotpotQA, ScoNe, HoVer, and PUPA - ESPO improves average accuracy by $+$3.76 pp over the state-of-the-art (74.67% vs 70.91% for GEPA), matching or exceeding GEPA on every dataset while producing prompts 47% shorter (1,004 vs 1,878 chars) and faster at inference. Cross-model experiments across four additional student models (Gemma 3 12B, Mistral 14B, Qwen3 32B, Claude Haiku 4.5) show ESPO yields the best average accuracy on every model tested, with the largest gap on Qwen3 GSM8K (15.00% $\to$ 91.40%). A generalization bound (Appendix) grounds each phase in a corresponding term of the test-time gap, and the ablation confirms a key prediction: adding diversity without bootstrap selection actually hurts performance ($-$1.20%).

</details>


### [177] [Compile by Training: Turning Natural-Language Specifications into Local Neural Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04199)
*Yuntian Deng,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"通过编译进行训练"(compile by training)方法，将自然语言规范转化为可重用的神经函数。编译时由教师模型生成任务特定示例，训练一个小型适配器用于紧凑解释器，使生成的函数无需教师模型即可运行，可像普通软件一样存储、版本化和组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重复性文本函数易于描述但难以用规则实现，而每次调用大型远程模型会带来重复成本、延迟和提供商依赖问题。

Method: 编译时由教师模型生成任务特定示例，用于训练紧凑解释器的小型适配器。函数编译完成后无需教师模型即可独立运行，可存储、版本化和组合。

Result: 在FuzzyBench-Hard基准上（Program-as-Weights快速编译器无精确匹配的子集），compile by training达到83.6%语义准确率，但编译时间成本更高（约一分钟而非数秒）。已在公共交互服务中部署，并在多站点网站助手、语言控制的3D虚拟化身和双向英语-Claudish翻译器中演示。

Conclusion: 该方法的更高准确率伴随更高的编译时成本，但在多种应用场景中展示了可行性和实用性。

Abstract: Many recurring text functions are easy to describe but difficult to implement with rules, while calling a large remote model for every input introduces repeated cost, latency, and dependency on a provider. We present compile by training, which turns a natural-language specification into a reusable neural function. At compile time, teacher models generate task-specific examples that are used to train a small adapter for a compact interpreter. The resulting function runs without the teachers and can be stored, versioned, and composed like ordinary software. On FuzzyBench-Hard, a subset on which the Program-as-Weights fast compiler produced no exact matches, compile by training reaches 83.6% semantic accuracy. This higher accuracy comes with a higher compile-time cost: roughly a minute rather than seconds for the fast compiler. We deploy the compiler in a public interactive service and demonstrate compiled functions in a multi-site website helper, a language-controlled 3D avatar, and a bidirectional English-Claudish translator.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型的unembedding矩阵中存在一个编码训练语料unigram分布的"无知方向"(direction of ignorance),该方向作为模型在不确定时的贝叶斯先验。通过将最终预测状态投影到此方向,可得到逐token的先验载荷因子λ,该因子随上下文信息量增加而下降,并对应tempered Bayesian update的两个正交因子。研究表明λ在不同模型规模和家族间具有可比性,且该方向具有因果活性——调整λ可引导模型预测朝向或远离unigram先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型在信息不足时如何做出预测,揭示模型内部的先验结构及其与贝叶斯更新的关系,从而深入理解模型的内在推理机制。

Method: 通过在四种模型家族(Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia,参数范围0.4B至405B)中分析unembedding矩阵的几何结构,识别编码unigram分布的单一方向;将最终预测状态投影到此方向以获得先验载荷因子λ;并通过tempered Bayesian update框架分解预测状态为正交的先验项和上下文似然项;最后通过因果干预实验验证该方向的功能。

Result: "无知方向"出现在所有四种模型家族中;λ随上下文信息增加而稳定下降;投影分解精确对应tempered Bayesian update的两个因子;较大模型在高上下文限制下普遍表现出较低的先验依赖;调整λ可因果性地引导预测朝向或远离unigram先验(KL散度度量)。

Conclusion: 语言模型的预测行为可以几何-概率地解释为tempered Bayesian update,其中unigram先验存储在unembedding方向的单一结构中。该发现不仅提供了模型不确定性的可解释视角,还揭示了可跨模型比较的先验依赖度量λ,并展示了该结构的因果可操作性。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [179] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"方程重铸"(equation recast)方法，将参数化算子学习重构为学习单一规范算子( canonical operator)的任务。参数引起的算子变化通过控制方程解析推导并吸收为等效源项，从而实现对全新参数范围的零样本预测。该方法在非线性、奇异PDE和核聚变托卡马克仿真中展现了外推能力，并利用收敛性损失作为内部警告信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯粹数据驱动的参数化神经PDE模型需要在输入函数和物理参数上大量覆盖训练数据，成本高昂，且模型在训练分布之外可能无预警地失败。这限制了神经算子求解器的泛化性、可重用性和可靠性。

Method: 1) 从控制方程出发，将参数诱导的算子变化解析地推导出来，并吸收为"等效源项"；2) 将不同物理参数下的学习问题统一映射为单一规范算子的学习；3) 支持通过规范表示整合稀疏异构数据集；4) 使用重铸迭代的收敛性丧失作为内部失败的预警信号。

Result: 在多参数、非线性和奇异PDE场景中实现了跨参数外推；在核聚变托卡马克高保真仿真中，通过规范域映射在一个联合训练的算子内统一了四种设备几何的电子温度数据。

Conclusion: 方程重铸为可重用的神经PDE求解器提供了路径，实现了方程引导的迁移、数据效率和可监控的推理，增强了模型在训练分布之外的可靠性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [180] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综合性综述，系统探讨了从欧几里得数据到图结构化数据的协作学习领域，涵盖其基本原理、分布场景分类、问题形式化、算法框架及开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习（集中式数据收集、训练和推理）面临可扩展性和隐私等根本性限制。虽然联邦学习和去中心化学习等协作学习方法已被探索，但大多数研究集中在欧几里得数据（如图像、文本）上，对图结构化数据在协作环境中的学习研究仍然不足。

Method: 该综述首先按学习有效性、效率和隐私保护三个核心维度回顾了欧几里得数据协作学习的基本原则；随后扩展到图结构化数据，引入了图分布场景的分类法，表征了相关的统计异质性，并开发了标准化的问题表述和算法框架。

Result: 构建了一个从欧几里得到图数据的协作学习综合框架，提出了图分布场景的taxonomy，并系统化了图协作学习中的问题定义与算法体系。

Conclusion: 系统地识别了该领域开放挑战和有前景的研究方向，为该新兴领域的研究提供了整合性基础。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [181] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于经验证据的混合注意力架构设计方法。通过两种干预指标（RFIS和RPD），在RoPE-based Transformer中发现检索头与位置头的完整分类学，由全局位置带（GPBand）划分。据此提出头级混合架构HwH，使用NoPE全注意力处理全局检索、线性注意力处理局部位置建模，在低FA:LA比例下保持强语言建模能力，同时显著提升检索和零样本长上下文外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全注意力与线性注意力的混合架构日益重要，但两者之间的资源分配仍依赖启发式方法，缺乏证据基础。作者希望通过理解RoPE-based Transformer中头级功能组织来指导混合架构设计。

Method: 提出两种干预指标：(1) RFIS（RoPE频率重要性分数）衡量每个频率对注意力分布的影响；(2) RPD（RoPE位置依赖度）隔离对旋转位置调制的依赖。在Qwen3和Llama3.1上验证，发现检索头与位置头由中低频带的显著边界（GPBand）分离。据此设计HwH架构：NoPE全注意力负责全局检索，线性注意力负责局部位置建模，并在头级粒度进行层特定分配。

Result: HwH在FA:LA比例低于1:3时，保留强语言建模和常识推理能力，同时改善检索性能，并在零样本长上下文外推上显著优于Transformer、线性注意力和层级混合基线。消融实验验证了两条设计原则和各组件的作用。

Conclusion: 位置建模应仅在局部进行，全局访问通过位置无关的检索实现；两种功能应在头级粒度以层特定方式分配。有原则的混合架构设计是未来基础模型发展的有前景方向，该分析还揭示了零样本长度外推失败的潜在原因。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [182] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL)，一种直接优化生成式策略在高奖励尾部覆盖率的强化学习方法。TailRL通过最大化策略超过随机选取奖励阈值的对数概率，使其梯度更重视稀有但高奖励的rollout，可解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合。该方法仅需简单修改advantage函数，即可兼容现有RL流程，并在对象定位、迷宫导航、GUI接地和代码优化等任务中展现出优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习优化平均奖励，但对于生成式策略，平均奖励掩盖了一个关键问题：两个策略可能具有相同的平均奖励，但产生稀有高奖励rollout的概率差异巨大。随着训练和推理时采样数量的增加，保留高奖励结果的概率质量会带来显著收益，但平均奖励优化无法捕捉这一点。

Method: TailRL将连续的奖励视为一族二元成功事件（每个奖励阈值对应一个事件），通过最大化策略超过随机选取奖励阈值的对数概率来直接优化覆盖率。其梯度天然地对稀有高奖励rollout赋予更大权重，并且可以解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合。在实现上，TailRL只需要对advantage函数进行简单修改，即可与现有强化学习流水线兼容。

Result: 在对象定位、迷宫导航、GUI接地和代码优化等多种任务上，TailRL能够利用稀有高奖励训练样本避免次优解，并产生在推理时受益于更多额外采样的模型。

Conclusion: 通过直接优化高奖励尾部覆盖率而非仅平均奖励，TailRL为生成式策略提供了更优的优化目标，使模型能够更好地保留高奖励概率质量，从而在训练和推理阶段更好地利用采样带来的收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [183] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理信息的图注意力网络（GAT）代理模型，直接在四面体TCAD网格上预测每个网格节点处的静电势及电子/空穴准费米能级（漂移-扩散系统的三个基本未知量），并结合有限体积电流连续性残差进行训练。该模型具有尺寸泛化能力，配合深度集成的逐节点不确定性和主动学习循环，可在数秒内筛选海量候选设计，仅将最有信息量的设计提交全量模拟。在多个鳍三栅FinFET上与Sentaurus Device基准对比，代理模型逐场RMSE低于1V，每设计吞吐量高出若干数量级，且优势随器件规模增大而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD漂移-扩散模拟计算代价高昂，尤其3D结构下运行时随网格复杂度急剧上升，严重限制多目标设计空间探索。现有机器学习代理模型将固定设计参数映射到少量标量器件指标，丢弃底层物理，缺乏跨几何体和器件族的迁移能力。

Method: 构建物理信息图注意力网络：直接在四面体TCAD网格上作为图操作，逐节点预测静电势及电子/空穴准费米能级（漂移-扩散系统基本未知量）。训练目标结合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差，将载流子输运物理嵌入目标函数。网格即图的结构使模型继承尺寸泛化能力，少鳍网格训练的模型可直接用于更大阵列，推理仅受GPU内存限制。深度集成的逐节点不确定性驱动主动学习循环，快速筛选候选池并将最有信息量的设计转交全量模拟。

Result: 在多方栅FinFET上与Sentaurus Device对比：代理模型按场RMSE低于每场1V，每设计吞吐量比全模拟器高出数量级。优势随器件规模增长，对直接模拟代价过高的多鳍阵列，仍可在每器件不到一秒内完成推理，实现跨器件尺度的Pareto前沿探索。

Conclusion: 该物理信息GAT代理模型通过在网格级预测漂移-扩散基本场变量并嵌入物理约束，实现了准确、尺寸可迁移且高吞吐的器件仿真替代，使原本不可行的跨尺度TCAD扫描式Pareto探索成为可能。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [184] [Risk and Anomaly Identification for Distribution Network Optimal Operation Based on Reinforcement Learning and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03308)
*Ziqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向现代配电网的深度强化学习（DRL）框架，通过融合分布性与贝叶斯DRL实现二阶不确定性量化，将总不确定性分解为偶然（aleatoric）与认知（epistemic）两部分，分别用于刻画固有运行风险与分布外异常检测，并在部署阶段支持回退控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代配电网的可靠运行要求在普遍不确定性下及时识别运行风险与异常事件。实际中，需要区分两类问题：随机但在分布（in-distribution）条件下固有的风险，以及对应分布外（out-of-distribution）行为的异常（如异常负荷模式、极端天气或网络物理攻击）。现有方法难以同时处理这两种识别任务。

Method: 提出不确定性感知的深度强化学习框架。核心方法是整合分布性DRL与贝叶斯DRL，实现二阶不确定性量化方案，将总不确定性分解为偶然不确定性与认知不确定性两个分量：前者用于刻画固有运行风险，后者用于表征分布外异常。认知估计在训练阶段驱动探索，在部署阶段用于分布外检测并触发回退控制。

Result: 仿真结果表明所提出的DRL智能体具有良好的性能表现，且不确定性量化方案有效。

Conclusion: 通过二阶不确定性分解，该框架能够同时实现配电网固有风险的评估与分布外异常的检测与应对，为不确定性环境下的配电网优化运行提供了一种统一且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable operation of modern distribution networks requires timely identification of operational risks and anomalous events under pervasive uncertainty. In practice, operators must identify risks that are inherent in stochastic yet in-distribution conditions, and anomalies that correspond to out-of-distribution behaviors such as unusual load patterns, extreme weather or cyber-physical attacks. This paper addresses this joint risk and anomaly identification problem for optimal distribution network operation and proposes a deep reinforcement learning framework that is explicitly uncertainty aware. We integrate distributional and Bayesian deep reinforcement learning to realize a second- order uncertainty quantification scheme that decomposes total uncertainty into aleatoric and epistemic components, which are respectively used to characterize inherent risk and out-of- distribution anomalies. The resulting epistemic estimates drive both exploration during training and out-of-distribution detec- tion with fallback control during deployment, whereas aleatoric estimates are used to characterize intrinsic operational risk. Simulation results demonstrate the performance of our DRL agent and the effectiveness of the uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [185] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是一种图网络模拟器，通过在接触边上直接存储交互历史来模拟颗粒动力学，采用注意力消息传递和门控循环单元维护边记忆，并使用边身份字典在接触图变化时保留记忆。在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准测试中，相比现有方法将长滚动位置误差降低31-62%，最终堆积误差降低58-89%，并实现比MPM参考求解器8.9-12.2倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒运动强烈依赖颗粒间接触历史，但接触的形成、断裂和重排使得历史信息难以保留。现有模拟器主要将时间信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆中，未能充分捕获接触层面的历史交互信息。

Method: TRACE将交互历史直接存储在接触边上：每条边使用注意力消息传递和门控循环单元维持持久记忆；边身份字典在接触图动态变化时保留记忆；物理结构化解码器预测法向和切向接触力，强制执行库仑摩擦界限并施加等大反向内力；采用单步预训练加自回归推演微调的训练策略。

Result: 在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准上，TRACE产生稳定且物理一致的长时滚动，准确再现最终堆积几何和坍塌释放的动能。相比GNS和NMGNS，长滚动位置误差降低31-62%，最终堆积误差降低58-89%，参数更少且颗粒几乎无相互穿透。相比MPM求解器在2D和3D分别实现12.2倍和8.9倍加速。

Conclusion: 在接触边上存储交互历史是提高颗粒动力学图网络模拟器精度的有效策略，TRACE在精度、参数效率和计算速度上均优于现有方法，为学习型颗粒模拟提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [186] [Inferred Generative-Process Diversity Predicts Correlated Failure Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03422)
*Ross Tieman,Evan Markou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"生成过程多样性"作为多语言模型系统中比语义多样性更根本的多样性概念，利用归一化压缩距离（NCD）结合置换控制来推断生成过程多样性。在38个语言模型和10个基准任务上的实验表明，该指标比语义相似度更能预测模型对的去相关化失败，增加生成过程多样性可减少相关失败，为多模型系统的安全评估提供了新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型组成的集体系统中，不同模型常被视为独立组件，但即使它们的输出在语义上高度相似或被判定为不同，其行为和失败模式可能仍然强相关。现有的语义相似度评估只能捕捉输出含义的差异，无法揭示更深层的、导致相关失败的模型多样性差异，需要一种更根本的多样性度量方式。

Method: 作者从算法信息论出发，提出"生成过程多样性"概念，使用原始模型输出之间的归一化压缩距离（NCD），并以置换控制进行残差化处理，作为推断生成过程多样性的度量。研究涵盖38个语言模型，在10个不相交的基准任务家族上检验该指标对跨任务变化的去相关化相关失败的预测能力，同时控制语义相似度和模型能力对结果的影响。

Result: 该指标揭示了语义相似度未能捕捉的群体结构。跨基准的部分秩关联为-0.216（95%区间[-0.309,-0.122]），且该估计在全部10个基准上均为负值，表明生成过程多样性增加与模型对相关失败减少显著相关，且这一效应不能归因于语义相似度或能力差异。

Conclusion: 推断的生成过程多样性提供了一种新颖且实用的方法来研究多模型系统在安全相关情境中的多样性，能够识别语义相似度遗漏的群体结构，并对模型间的相关失败做出超越语义和能力指标的预测。

Abstract: Diversity is a widely observed factor in the resilient function of collective systems, yet the type of diversity that matters depends on the properties and failure modes of the system. This distinction is important for systems composed of multiple language models. Different models may be treated as independent components even when their behaviour and failures remain strongly correlated. Assessments of language-model populations using semantic similarity demonstrate limited semantic diversity, but this captures only differences in the meaning of observed outputs. We argue that a more fundamental notion of model diversity is generative-process diversity, the differences between processes capable of generating the observed outputs. Drawing from Algorithmic Information Theory, we use Normalised Compression Distance between raw model outputs, residualised against a permutation control, as a measure of inferred generative-process diversity. Across 38 language models, this measure identifies population structure missed by semantic similarity and predicts cross-task variation in chance-corrected correlated failure among model pairs across ten disjoint benchmark families, beyond semantic similarity and model-pair capability. The cross-benchmark partial rank association is $-0.216$ with a 95% interval of $[-0.309,-0.122]$, and the estimate is negative on all ten benchmarks. These results indicate that increased generative-process diversity is associated with reduced correlated failure in model pairs that is not attributable to semantic similarity or capability. Inferred generative-process diversity offers a novel and practical approach for investigating diversity of multi-model systems in safety-relevant contexts.

</details>


### [187] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有限库输入扭曲贝叶斯优化（FLIWBO），通过从有限的平滑输入映射库中选择扭曲映射来适应输入几何结构，在保持高斯过程上置信界（GP-UCB）高概率收敛保证的同时，加速学习并提升样本效率。实验证明其在错配几何下优于原始坐标GP-UCB，并能逃离传统方法的陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-BO在超参数优化和多智能体系统设计中表现出色，其GP-UCB方法虽具收敛保证但要求固定核函数。然而，当原始坐标与目标函数的几何结构不匹配时（如对数尺度超参数或局部峰值），输入扭曲可大幅提升样本效率，但现有GP-UCB证明无法适用于扭曲后的动态核函数。因此需要一种既能自适应输入几何又保留收敛保证的方法。

Method: FLIWBO从有限库中选择平滑输入映射进行输入扭曲，允许使用任意历史依赖的规则选择扭曲映射。通过限制扭曲映射为有限库中的元素，在温和假设下保留了高概率收敛保证，并给出了显式的库大小成本√(N_ε)。文中结合GP-UCB形成FLIWBO-UCB算法进行实验验证。

Result: 有限库扭曲能够修复人为设置的几何错配问题，并识别出FLIWBO的失败情形。在四个基准测试中（扭曲合成目标、置信围栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST HPO），FLIWBO-UCB在几何错配下优于原始坐标GP-UCB，能逃离甚至击败oracle扭曲期望改进的陷阱，并恢复了手动对数缩放的大部分增益。20维MAS设计研究进一步验证了在昂贵噪声评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO在自适应输入几何与收敛保证之间取得了有效平衡，为GP-BO在实际应用中对几何错配的鲁棒性提供了理论基础和实践验证。有限库方法以√(N_ε)的成本代价换取了灵活性，是该领域的显著进步。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [188] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电子海图（ENC）空间矢量数据表示为图结构，将更新分类问题建模为图对分类任务，并评估多种GNN架构对导航安全关键性变更的分类效果，结果表明基于图的表示能提升ENC更新分类性能，可扩展自动化维护流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子海图（ENC）是用于海上导航的地理空间矢量数据集，维护这些数据具有挑战性。尤其是根据变更对导航安全的重要性对ENC中对象变更进行分类尤为关键。然而，目前尚不清楚哪种消息传递操作、架构配置和图表示最适合此任务。

Method: 将空间矢量导航数据表示为图结构：空间对象作为节点，其空间和语义关系构成边。将旧ENC数据集和新ENC数据集各编码为一对图，将该任务建模为图对分类问题。在此表示基础上，研究使用多种GNN架构（消息传递操作和模型配置）来分类所编码的图对是否构成对导航安全的关键或非关键风险。

Result: 在由海事专家评审的ENC变更数据集上训练和评估了多种GNN架构和模型配置。结果表明，基于图的表示能够改善ENC更新的分类性能。

Conclusion: 基于图的深度学习方法为自动化或改进ENC维护工作流程提供了一种可扩展的方案，证明了图神经网络在图结构化的空间数据分类任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [189] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TGOPD是一种教师门控的在线策略蒸馏方法，通过在提示级别验证教师可靠性来决定使用密集OPD监督还是基于验证器的GRPO，在4B和35B规模的数学、代码和指令跟随任务上均优于普通OPD，并将异步OPD中教师节点的GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通在线策略蒸馏（Vanilla OPD）对所有提示统一应用密集的token级教师监督，未检查教师在每个提示上的可靠性。由于反向KL是模式寻求的，自信但错误的教师会产生强烈且误导性的更新。现有的分布性代理指标（如熵或师生似然一致性）只能衡量不确定性或一致性，无法直接验证结果正确性。

Method: TGOPD基于"教师可靠性应在允许密集监督前于提示级别进行验证"的原则。首先用少量验证器评分的教师探测输出来估计可靠性，然后根据可靠性检查结果将每个提示路由到两种路径之一：通过检查则使用密集OPD，否则使用基于验证器的GRPO。

Result: 在4B和35B学生模型上，TGOPD在数学、代码和指令跟随的全部六个单领域设置中均优于Vanilla OPD；在多领域训练下，两个规模均达到更高的七基准平均值。此外，TGOPD利用原本闲置的教师容量进行可靠性估计，在测量的4B单领域运行中，将教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提高到78.9%。

Conclusion: TGOPD通过提示级别的可靠性门控，有效避免了错误教师的误导性密集更新，同时充分利用了异步OPD中闲置的教师算力，在多个任务和规模上取得了一致的性能提升，验证了"先验证、后监督"这一设计原则的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [190] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为具有转移不确定性的通用和并发随机博弈（CSGs）提出了PAC学习框架，在解决纳什均衡存在性挑战的同时，通过数据驱动的L1置信集和鲁棒MDP探索机制，实现社会福利最优的ε-近似纳什均衡学习，并引入纳什边际特征化来实现均衡存在性的原则性推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用和并发随机博弈中的纳什均衡学习面临两大核心挑战：(1) 转移不确定性下的学习复杂性；(2) 纳什均衡本身可能不存在的问题。现有工作未能在统一的PAC框架下同时处理这两个问题。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 算法维护数据驱动的L^1置信集来刻画转移核的不确定性，求解鲁棒CSG以获得社会福利最优的ε-NE；采用基于鲁棒MDP的探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖；引入纳什边际特征化，使框架要么返回ε-近似NE且社会福利值接近最优，要么提供不存精确NE的可靠证书。

Result: 在最小可达性条件p_reach>0下，算法在多项式数量的轨迹样本后终止，样本复杂度为Õ(R_max² H⁴ |S|² |A| / (p_reach ε²))。基准CSG上的实验表明：近似最优性能、正确处理均衡存在/不存在，以及样本复杂度与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统地统一了CSG中的PAC学习与均衡存在性验证问题，所提出的纳什边际特征化为处理均衡（不）存在性提供了理论支撑，样本复杂度分析具有实际意义。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


### [191] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对因果基础模型（CFMs）这一新兴领域提供实用导论。CFMs将基础模型的范式引入因果推断：预训练后的神经网络能够通过上下文学习直接估计新数据集上的因果量（如平均处理效应），无需针对每个新问题进行定制化流程或模型更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断需要为每个新问题定制流程：提出因果机制、选择估计器、训练模型，成本高昂且缺乏灵活性。与此同时，机器学习已普遍转向基础模型范式（大规模预训练+零样本/少样本迁移）。本文旨在将这一成功范式引入因果推断领域。

Method: 本文是综述/导论性质的工作，首先总结因果推断和机器学习所需的背景知识，然后系统介绍CFMs的概念与原理，并提供示例代码和Jupyter notebooks作为实践指南。

Result: 为实践者提供了CFMs的完整入门路径，涵盖背景知识、核心概念以及可运行的代码示例，使研究者能够快速上手并应用这一新兴方法。

Conclusion: CFMs代表了因果推断领域向基础模型范式迈进的趋势，有望大幅降低因果估计的定制化成本，实现跨任务、跨数据集的通用因果推断能力。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [192] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ObserverBench 是一个用于评估内部估计器（observer）是否足以指导干预、控制或安全任务的基准框架。核心观点是：平均准确但不一定导致好的行动决策，因此需要将估计准确性与行动损失分开报告。论文通过理论分析和在 GPT-2-small、Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it 等多个模型上的实验证明了两者缺一不可，并提供了固定任务合同、可运行基线和基于表格的提交格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性越来越多地用于引导干预（如激活导向、电路移除、安全监控），但一个平均意义上准确的内部估计器仍可能选择糟糕的行动。现有基准只关注估计准确性，忽略了行动层面的后果，导致评估与实际用途脱节。

Method: 构建 ObserverBench 框架，每个任务固定模型、信息边界、允许动作、决策规则、留出案例和损失函数。基准将估计准确性与所选行动造成的损失分开报告。通过理论分析（闭环控制中 observer 误差的影响）、电路干预任务、安全分流任务等实验验证两者缺一不可的必要性。

Result: 在电路干预任务中，成对 observer 能更准确预测未见效果但不总能选更好行动；基于行动损失训练的 observer 能选更低损失的行动。在安全分流中，能完美区分违规行为的分数可能因违规成本不同而低效分配预算。AUROC 排名与部署损失排名不同，最佳信息源随模型变化。稀疏 SAE 读出在 Qwen 面板上落后于层匹配的稠密对照。

Conclusion: 评估可解释性方法应考虑其启用的行动质量，而非仅关注估计准确性。ObserverBench 提供标准化任务契约和可运行基线，使内部估计器的充分性可以在其导向的干预任务中进行公平且可复现的评估。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [193] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentDDM提出了一种协同设计预训练与迁移的方法：先在小子域上预训练神经算子，迁移到新场景时冻结该算子，仅训练轻量级组合模块将局部预测拼接为全局场，从而大幅降低部署时对高保真仿真数据的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为物理仿真的快速可微替代品，在训练分布外的几何、尺度或工况下精度急剧下降。监督式自适应虽能恢复精度，但即便很小的目标数据集也需昂贵的仿真代价。作者认为应协同设计预训练与迁移策略以降低部署成本，而非分别独立处理。

Method: LatentDDM分两阶段：1) 在小尺寸子域上预训练神经算子以预测局部物理场；2) 面对新设置时，冻结预训练算子只训练轻量模块，该模块负责组合（compose）局部预测结果形成完整全局预测，实现跨尺度与跨工况的迁移。

Result: 在稳态Darcy流（需跨越来越大多孔域的远程压力耦合）与绕俯仰翼型非定常不可压流（目标俯仰频率超出训练范围）两个互补任务上：相比容量匹配的全域同时处理模型，LatentDDM在更大Darcy域上经16个目标仿真自适应后误差降低36-56%；在快速俯仰翼型流中，20步场滚动预测在零样本与少样本校准后均有提升。

Conclusion: 实验表明"局部预训练+组合层迁移"的协同设计是物理基础模型极具前景的设计原则，能有效降低跨几何、尺度与工况部署时的自适应成本。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [194] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanStream是一个面向端侧设备LLM推理的流式"推测-精炼"（speculate-and-refine）框架，通过利用部分GPU结果逐步精炼计算、加载和缓存保留优先级，实现GPU执行与存储I/O的细粒度重叠，在移动和嵌入式平台上相比现有系统将内存占用降低4.8倍至7.5倍，同时将token生成吞吐量提升1.6倍至2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧LLM推理对隐私和响应速度至关重要，但模型权重远超设备DRAM容量。现有系统利用激活稀疏性并将权重卸载到SSD或闪存，却面临根本性的系统权衡：精确的稀疏执行决策需要最新的上下文信息，而高效的计算-I/O重叠又需要提前预测，导致传统设计要么串行执行、要么产生冗余权重获取、额外计算和巨大的缓存开销。

Method: LeanStream采用流式"推测-精炼"框架，利用部分GPU计算结果渐进式地精炼计算、加载和缓存保留（cache-retention）的优先级，使得GPU执行与存储I/O之间能够实现细粒度的重叠，从而打破"准确决策"与"高效重叠"之间的两难困境。

Result: 在移动和嵌入式平台上实现。与先前的端侧LLM推理系统相比，在先前工作达到最优吞吐量的条件下，LeanStream将内存使用量降低4.8倍至7.5倍，同时进一步将token生成吞吐量提升1.6倍至2.1倍。

Conclusion: LeanStream通过渐进式精炼策略有效解决了端侧LLM推理中稀疏执行决策准确性与计算-I/O高效重叠之间的根本矛盾，在显著降低内存占用的同时提升了token生成吞吐量，为端侧大模型推理提供了更优的系统设计方案。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is attractive for privacy and responsiveness, but remains challenging on mobile and embedded devices because model weights far exceed available DRAM. Prior systems exploit activation sparsity and offload weights to SSD or flash storage, but face a fundamental systems trade-off: accurate sparse execution decisions require the latest context, whereas efficient computation-I/O overlap requires early prediction. As a result, existing designs either serialize execution or incur redundant weight fetches, extra computation, and large cache overheads. We present LeanStream, a streaming speculate-and-refine framework for efficient on-device LLM inference. LeanStream progressively refines computation, loading, and cache-retention priorities using partial GPU results, enabling fine-grained overlap between GPU execution and storage I/O. We implement LeanStream on both mobile and embedded platforms. Compared with prior on-device LLM inference systems, LeanStream reduces memory usage by 4.8$\times$ to 7.5$\times$ at the best throughput achieved by prior work, while further improving token generation throughput by 1.6$\times$ to 2.1$\times$.

</details>


### [195] [The Gradient Does Not See Rank: Rank-Indifference in Matrix-CODI on ProsQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03090)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了矩阵链式思维(Matrix-CODI)模型中潜在矩阵Z的秩是否承载可解释的推理结构。通过秩-k投影消融实验，作者发现无论何种训练范式、何种读出层(readout)设计，秩-k消融曲线都保持平坦（精度差异在0.6个百分点内），表明该模型并不将并行推理路径编码在矩阵的秩中。进一步实验表明，这种"秩盲性"(rank-blindness)并非源于读出层的线性限制，而是秩-k消融本身混淆了"秩盲性"与"位置无关性"——在无矩阵瓶颈的GPT-2负对照中也观察到同样的平坦曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续链式思维模型将推理压缩为潜在token，矩阵变体通过d×d矩阵瓶颈路由每个潜在token，从而引入秩作为单样本结构可观测量。如果矩阵潜在表示通过叠加(superposition)承载并行推理路径，那么秩应当追踪这些路径，将Z截断到低秩应损害需要多组分解空间的复杂任务的准确率。作者旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 在四种训练范式（三种ProsQA、一种GSM8K-Aug低于学习阈值）上训练矩阵-CODI模型，执行秩-k投影消融实验；随后训练四种替代读出层（双线性重参数化、双线性+GELU非线性、SVD增强MLP、ZZ^T二次读出）重复实验；补充线性探针比较和目标预测AUC分析；最后在无矩阵瓶颈的GPT-2 SFT上做负对照，引入随机-h敏感性下界检验。

Result: 秩-k投影消融曲线在所有训练范式和读出层设计中均平坦（差异≤0.6pp）；三种子复现精度81.0±2.0pp而有效秩跨度{4,12,13}，损失不奖励特定秩；Spearman p值（0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46）均不显著；线性探针AUC 0.673劣于原始预训练隐状态0.846；GPT-2负对照复现平坦曲线（池化均值范围0.20pp），与随机-h敏感性下界一致。

Conclusion: 矩阵-CODI模型中的潜在矩阵秩不编码结构化的并行推理路径——模型对秩呈系统性盲性。该秩盲性不仅存在于线性读出层，也存在于对Z非线性的读出层。更根本的是，秩-k消融作为一种干预手段本身无法区分"模型确实不使用秩"与"秩在该位置根本无关"这两种情况，必须借助位置相关干预（如h向量的逐位置矩阵）和负对照来甄别。矩阵链式思维中的秩可能只是优化副产品而非可解释计算结构。

Abstract: Continuous chain-of-thought models compress reasoning into latent tokens. Matrix-valued variants, which route each latent token through a d x d matrix bottleneck, introduce rank as a single-sample structural observable on the latent matrix Z. If matrix latents carry parallel reasoning paths via superposition, rank should track them, and truncating Z to low rank should hurt accuracy on tasks whose solutions plausibly require multiple components. Across four training regimes of a matrix-CODI model (three on ProsQA, one on GSM8K-Aug below the learning threshold), the rank-k projection ablation curve is flat to within 0.6 percentage points. A three-seed replication yields 81.0 +/- 2.0 percentage points accuracy while the final effective rank of Z spans {4, 12, 13}; the loss does not reward any particular rank. To test whether rank-blindness arises from the flatten-then-project readout alone, we trained four readouts: a bilinear reparametrization, a bilinear-plus-GELU readout nonlinear in Z, an SVD-augmented readout feeding singular values through an MLP, and a quadratic readout in Z Z^T. All four rank-k curves remain flat (Spearman p-values 0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46). The flat curves persist for readouts nonlinear in Z. A linear probe on Z underperforms a raw pretrained hidden state at target prediction (AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846). A negative control on vanilla GPT-2 SFT (no matrix bottleneck, no Z, three seeds, n=500) reproduces a flat rank-k curve under the same intervention paradigm with pooled-mean range 0.20pp, and a random-h sensitivity floor lands at the same accuracy: the rank-k ablation alone conflates rank-blindness with position-irrelevance.

</details>


### [196] [Distilling deep optical flow stereo methods to retrieve dense three-dimensional wind fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03100)
*Thomas J. Vandal,Dong L. Wu,James L. Carr,Derek J. Posselt,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Kate Duffy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用深度光流替代传统窗口相关法进行立体匹配，实现静止卫星大气运动矢量（AMV）的高效高精度反演。通过自监督几何残差损失与监督无线电探空重建的微调，并将立体教师模型蒸馏为单卫星学生模型，实现全球全盘辐照度图像上的风场反演，无需多卫星重叠与NWP依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AMV依赖窗口交叉相关跟踪与红外亮温结合NWP背景估计高度，导致循环依赖、高度不准、计算昂贵且反演稀疏。立体风虽能通过视差几何解算高度消除NWP依赖，但计算量仍大且覆盖有限。本文旨在用深度学习光流替代传统方法，实现高效、全球覆盖且不依赖NWP的风场反演。

Method: 使用深度光流替代立体匹配中的窗口跟踪；微调机制结合自监督几何残差损失与监督无线电探空重建；将立体（多卫星）教师模型蒸馏为单卫星学生模型以消除多卫星重叠需求；学生模型模拟教师的卡方分布与高度不确定性以进行质量保证。

Result: 通过三重配准验证显示，立体风在水汽波段（6.2、6.9和7.3微米）上性能优于业务AMV，但在长波红外（11.2微米）波段存在性能退化。学生模型可在全球范围内生成全盘地球同步卫星影像的风场。

Conclusion: 深度光流结合知识蒸馏能够有效替代传统AMV反演方法，在不依赖NWP的前提下提高水汽波段风场精度，并实现单卫星全球覆盖的实用性突破，但长波红外波段的性能退化仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Geostationary atmospheric motion vectors (AMVs) provide the dense horizontal wind vectors (u,v) and heights ingested into data assimilation systems. Traditional AMVs track features using window-based cross-correlation and estimate heights via infrared brightness temperatures paired with numerical weather prediction (NWP) background states, creating a circular dependency that yields inaccurate heights, high computational cost, and sparse retrievals. Stereo winds from GEO-GEO and GEO-LEO geometrically resolve heights from parallax shifts across different poses, eliminating NWP dependence and improving accuracy, but they remain computationally heavy with limited coverage. In this work, we replace window-based tracking in stereo matching with deep optical flow for efficient, improved retrieval. Fine-tuning balances a self-supervised geometric residual loss with supervised radiosonde reconstruction. To eliminate multi-satellite overlap requirements, we distill the stereo teacher into a single-satellite student model. Chi-square and height uncertainties from the teacher are emulated by the student for quality assurance. The student generates winds across full-disk GEO imagery globally. Validation compares stereo and student models against radiosondes, operational AMVs, ERA5 reanalysis, and EarthCARE cloud profiles. Results through triple collocation show that stereo winds improve performance beyond operational AMVs for water vapor bands (6.2, 6.9, and 7.3 μm), wit degradation in the long-wave infrared (11.2 μm) band.

</details>


### [197] [Scaling Laws, Tabular Data and Actuarial Ratemaking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03106)
*Ronald Richman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了深度学习中的缩放定律是否适用于精算定价领域（表格数据）。基于真实汽车保险数据，训练多种模型族在不同数据量下的表现，发现各模型族均随数据增加而改善，但缩放指数差异显著，其中TabM的数据缩放能力明显优于表格Transformer和MLP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中缩放定律已在语言模型等场景被广泛验证，但精算定价领域的数据是表格型、异构且高噪声的，经典GLM仍是强基线。作者希望探究此类领域是否也存在类似的缩放规律，以及架构设计如何影响数据缩放和参数缩放的效率。

Method: 使用真实汽车保险组合数据，训练不同模型族（TabM、表格Transformer、MLP等），在不同训练数据比例下及多个随机种子下进行实验，以样本外泊松偏差（Poisson deviance）作为评估指标，比较各模型族的数据缩放指数和参数缩放特性。

Result: 所有模型族都随数据增加而提升，但缩放指数差异明显：TabM的数据缩放能力显著强于纯监督式表格Transformer和标准MLP。Transformer变体在缺乏额外归纳偏置（TabM式适配或自监督）时参数缩放表现弱。

Conclusion: 在精算表格任务上有效缩放取决于架构和损失函数目标设计，单纯增加Transformer规模带来的增益有限。研究为不同数据规模下模型选择提供定量指导。

Abstract: Scaling laws in modern deep learning describe how held-out loss improves as model capacity, training data, and compute increase, often following power-law trends. We investigate whether analogous scaling regularities arise in actuarial ratemaking, where data are tabular, heterogeneous, and noisy, and where classical models such as GLMs remain strong baselines. Using a real-world motor insurance portfolio, we train models from different families across increasing fractions of the training data and multiple random seeds, evaluating out-of-sample Poisson deviance, a likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions in which lower values indicate better held-out fit. We find that all model families improve with additional data, but scaling exponents differ substantially: TabM exhibits markedly stronger data scaling than purely supervised tabular Transformers and standard MLP baselines. Transformer variants show weak parameter scaling unless augmented with additional inductive biases (TabM-style adaptation or self-supervision). These results provide quantitative guidance on model selection by data regime and suggest that effective scaling on actuarial tabular tasks depends on architecture and loss function objective design, with simple increases in Transformer size providing limited gains.

</details>


### [198] [Kernel Reboot: Breaking the Boundaries of Neural Tangent Kernels for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03117)
*Amir Mallak,Alaa Maalouf,Lior Wolf,Daniela Rus,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三种算法（NTK-KIP、MetaQuill、MetaQuill-KIP），将神经正切核（NTK）回归与元学习结合，使神经场（Neural Fields）在稀疏观测下实现快速、高质量的非线性重建与语义补全，同时仅需轻量级的实例适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场能从连续坐标映射到信号（如颜色或密度），但在稀疏观测下实现快速高质量重建仍具挑战。经典NTK回归虽能提供闭式解，但本质上是线性的，无法积累可复用的任务先验。

Method: 提出三种算法：(1) NTK-KIP：通过核诱导点（KIP）学习蒸馏的支撑坐标集，使有限NTK能从少量数据补全大块缺失区域；(2) MetaQuill：元学习INR的共享初始化，新场景仅需更新小规模任务专属权重偏移，实现真正的特征学习与可复用先验；(3) MetaQuill-KIP：融合两者，先用KIP风格的非线性热启动初始化任务，再围绕元学习初始化仅精调小偏移。

Result: MetaQuill-KIP在极稀疏观测下实现高PSNR重建和语义合理的补全，且仅需轻量级逐实例适配；相比之下，扩散式基线通常依赖大规模预训练生成先验和高代价的逐图像调优。

Conclusion: 研究表明NTK驱动的神经场可以实现非线性和元学习能力，缩小了解析核方法与实用小样本重建之间的差距。

Abstract: Neural fields (NFs) map continuous coordinates to signals such as color or density, but fast high-quality reconstruction from sparse observations remains difficult. Classical Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regression gives closed-form fits, yet it is fundamentally linear and cannot accumulate reusable task priors. We develop three algorithms that address these gaps. NTK-KIP learns a distilled support set of coordinates (and optional labels) so that a finite NTK can inpaint large missing regions from little observed data, yielding a compact non-linear representation instead of a raw kernel solve. MetaQuill meta-learns a shared initialization for an INR so that new scenes can be adapted by updating only a small task-specific weight offset, which provides true feature learning and a reusable prior. Finally, MetaQuill-KIP fuses both ideas: it seeds the task with a KIP-style non-linear warm start, then refines only that small offset around the meta-learned initialization. MetaQuill-KIP achieves high-PSNR reconstructions and semantically plausible inpainting under very sparse observations, while requiring only lightweight per-instance adaptation, whereas diffusion-style baselines typically depend on large pretrained generative priors and costly per-image tuning. This shows that NTK-driven neural fields can be made both non-linear and meta-learnable, narrowing the gap between analytic kernels and practical few-shot reconstruction.

</details>


### [199] [Routing Is Not Enough: Diagnosing Intra-Adapter Subspace Contention in MoE+LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03150)
*Mehreen Hossain Chowdhury,Nowshin Mahjabin,Ahmed Shafin Ruhan,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了MoE+LoRA中"token级路由分离可以避免负迁移"的假设，通过实验发现即使路由在领域间近乎不相交，仍会出现负迁移。作者提出Jaccard路由重叠与adapter梯度余弦相似度两个诊断指标，定位到干扰源于低秩adapter子空间内的梯度竞争，并提出SpawnLoRA，在检测到adapter级竞争时动态在MoE专家内部添加门控子adapter，有效减少了负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域微调中常假设MoE路由与LoRA结合时，token级路由能分离领域特定更新，但该假设未被充分验证。作者旨在检验此假设，并解决即使路由分离仍然存在的负迁移问题。

Method: 1) 使用Python代码+生物医学文本+数学推理进行实验验证路由分离假设；2) 提出Jaccard路由重叠和adapter梯度余弦相似度两个诊断指标；3) 提出SpawnLoRA方法：当检测到adapter级竞争时，在MoE专家内动态添加门控子adapter，保持路由器固定。

Result: 在Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct和OLMoE-1B-7B上，SpawnLoRA在多种混合设置下相比标准LoRA和rank-adaptive LoRA均更有效地减少了负迁移。实验证实了专家内部的结构分离带来的收益超过单独的路径路由或秩扩展。

Conclusion: 结构分离（SpawnLoRA）在MoE专家内部提供的收益超越了单纯的路由分离或秩扩展，表明解决负迁移需要超越token级路由层面，深入到adapter子空间内部的结构性处理。

Abstract: Multi-domain fine-tuning often combines MoE routing with LoRA, assuming that token-level routing separates domain-specific updates. We test this assumption in MoE+LoRA using Python code paired with biomedical text and mathematical reasoning. Although these domains show near-disjoint expert routing, adding biomedical data substantially increases code perplexity, indicating that routing separation alone may not prevent negative transfer. To localize the failure, we introduce Jaccard routing overlap and adapter-gradient cosine similarity, which measure expert sharing and update compatibility, respectively. These diagnostics indicate that interference arises mostly from nearly orthogonal domain gradients competing within the same low-rank adapter subspace. We address this issue with SpawnLoRA, which dynamically adds gated sub-adapters inside MoE experts when adapter-level contention is detected, while keeping the router fixed. We evaluate SpawnLoRA on Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct and OLMoE-1B-7B across multiple mixture settings and find that it effectively reduces negative transfer compared with standard and rank-adaptive LoRA. These results demonstrate that structural separation inside experts provides benefits beyond routing or rank expansion alone.

</details>


### [200] [Frontier LLMs are effective batch optimizers: Assessing reasoning models in continuous and discrete settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03177)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,David Graff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了当前前沿大语言模型（LLMs）作为批量优化器在连续和离散优化场景中的表现。研究发现，LLMs在数值测试函数上作为零样本批量优化器具有竞争力，但性能不如经典非LLM优化方法稳健；然而在语义丰富的离散空间中，LLMs表现出显著优势，这表明其批量优化行为在其预训练数据结构相似的离散空间中最为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLMs凭借大规模预训练已成为有吸引力的优化先验，能够应对多种优化设置。然而，现代推理型LLMs在批量优化场景中的有效性尚未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 系统性地评估当前一代前沿LLMs作为批量优化器在连续和离散两种优化设置中的表现，并与经典的非LLM优化方法进行对比分析，同时区分数值测试函数和语义丰富场景等不同优化环境。

Result: LLMs在数值测试函数上是具有竞争力的零样本批量优化器，但其性能相比经典非LLM优化方法较为脆弱（brittle）。然而在语义丰富的离散空间中，LLMs的表现显著优于其他方法，这与其预训练数据结构高度相似的情境有关。

Conclusion: LLMs的批量优化行为在与其预训练数据结构相似的离散空间中高度有效，但在连续数值优化中鲁棒性不足。这提示LLM优化先验的价值高度依赖问题空间的语义丰富程度和与预训练数据的结构相似性。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have become attractive priors for optimization due to their large-scale pretraining that enables them to navigate a variety of optimization settings. However, the effectiveness of modern reasoning LLMs in batch optimization settings remains underexplored. Here we investigate the performance of the current generation of frontier LLMs as batch optimizers in both continuous and discrete settings. We find that while LLMs are competitive zero-shot batch optimizers for numerical test functions, their performance is brittle compared to classical non-LLM optimization approaches. However, LLM priors are significantly better in semantically rich settings, indicating that their batch optimization behavior is highly effective when navigating and reasoning over the discrete spaces most similar in structure to their pretraining data.

</details>


### [201] [Portable Causal Fairness Across Synthetic Data Generator Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03180)
*Steven Golob,Sikha Pentyala,Martine De Cock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地验证了将公平性约束（以因果图边切割形式）从单一的DECAF非私有GAN框架移植到九种生成器（基于边际、GAN和扩散三大族，各有差分隐私变体）中的可行性与效果，发现该机制在所有生成器中均能有效转移，且新提出的因果扩散骨干网络在公平性上表现最佳，在保真度上接近边际层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DECAF框架提出了通过因果图边切割实现公平性约束的想法，但此前仅在一个非私有GAN上得到验证，无法确定该机制是否属于DECAF本身，还是因果因子分解这一核心思想固有的属性，需要跨生成器家族进行普适性检验。

Method: 将三种公平性定义移植到九种来自三个不相关家族（基于边际、GAN、扩散）的生成器上，每种均含差分隐私变体，在三个正式隐私保证级别下，于Adult和COMPAS数据集上进行2520次配对运行实验；同时提出新的因果扩散骨干网络作为生成器。

Result: 公平性切割机制在所有生成器上均成功转移；新提出的因果扩散骨干在公平性上优于所有已测家族，保真度接近边际层；施加切割对保真度几乎无影响，下游分类器AUC平均仅损失0.07到0.15；隐私保证不会降低数据的公平性。

Conclusion: 公平性边切割机制并非DECAF特有，而是因果因子分解的普适属性，可广泛应用于各种生成器家族；因果扩散生成器在公平-保真权衡上表现最优，且隐私保护与公平性可以兼得。

Abstract: When a statistical agency or regulator releases synthetic data in place of sensitive records, it chooses the generator that produces the table, and can shape that generator so unfair pathways are absent. DECAF made this concrete on one non-private GAN: three fairness definitions become three sets of edge cuts on the generator's causal graph. Whether the mechanism belongs to DECAF, or to causal factorisation itself, was untested. We port all three definitions to nine generators from three unrelated families (marginals-based, GAN, and diffusion, each with differentially private variants), across three levels of formal privacy guarantee, over 2,520 matched-pair runs on Adult and COMPAS datasets. The mechanism transfers everywhere, and our new causal diffusion backbone yields the fairest release of any family we tested, at fidelity close to the marginals tier. Applying the cut barely moves fidelity, only costs a downstream classifier about $0.07$ to $0.15$ AUC on average, and adding privacy guarantees don't make the data less fair.

</details>


### [202] [Language-encoded network topology enables large language models to reason about complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03229)
*Ucchwas Talukder Utsha,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Md Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioGlyph将网络拓扑编译为可解释、可迁移的结构角色语言，使LLM能更好回答网络结构推理问题。通过图分割和结构测量识别枢纽、社区核心和跨社区连接器等角色，用固定规则翻译成通用词汇。在20个网络、5个领域中，相比基于边、数值和学习到的表示，系统准确率提升最高达26个百分点。该增益显式编码结构角色而来，在密集、社区结构化网络中更明显。在酵母蛋白互作网络中，跨社区连接器富集必需基因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自然语言上表现优异，但当网络以边列表、句子或测量表形式呈现时，难以推理网络的结构含义（如中心性、桥接社区、删除元素后的变化）。需要一种将网络拓扑转化为LLM可理解的可解释表示的方法。

Method: BioGlyph结合图分割与结构测量来识别结构角色（如枢纽、社区核心、跨社区连接器），并利用固定规则将这些角色翻译成一种通用的、可解释且可迁移的词汇表。每个元素通过其结构角色、支撑证据和语义后果来描述，网络本身和LLM均无需修改。

Result: 在横跨5个领域的20个网络中，BioGlyph显著提升开放LLM回答结构推理问题的能力，相比基于边、数值及学习到的表示，系统准确率最高提升26个百分点。消融实验表明增益来自以语义可解释术语显式编码结构角色。增益在密集、社区结构化网络中更突出，在稀疏网络中减弱。在出芽酵母蛋白互作网络中，跨社区连接器富集必需基因，而外围蛋白则被耗减。

Conclusion: BioGlyph为LLM和科学家提供了一种可解释的网络结构推理表示，既提升了模型的结构性问答能力，又能揭示真实的生物组织特征（如必需基因的分布模式），展现了跨领域网络结构推理的通用价值。

Abstract: Networks describe systems in biology and beyond, from protein interactions and social relationships to power grids and citation records. Reasoning about such systems requires understanding their structure: which elements are central, which connections bridge separate communities, and how it changes when elements are removed. Although large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language, they struggle with such questions when networks are given as edge lists, sentences or measurement tables, because their structural meaning must be inferred. Here we introduce BioGlyph, which compiles network topology into an interpretable and transferable language of structural roles. BioGlyph combines graph partitioning and structural measurements to identify roles such as hubs, community cores and cross-community connectors, and fixed rules to translate them into a universal vocabulary. The representation describes each element through its structural role, supporting evidence and semantic consequences, leaving both the network and the LLM unchanged. Across twenty networks spanning five domains, BioGlyph substantially improves open LLMs' ability to answer structural reasoning questions, outperforming edge-based, numerical and learned representations by up to 26 percentage points in system accuracy. Ablations show that the gain comes from explicitly encoding structural roles in semantically interpretable terms. The gain is more prominent in dense, community-structured networks and diminishes in sparse networks whose topology is more readily inferred from text. In a budding-yeast protein-interaction network, BioGlyph exposes biological organization: cross-community connectors are enriched for essential genes, whereas peripheral proteins are depleted. BioGlyph thus provides an interpretable representation for both language models and scientists to reason about network structure.

</details>


### [203] [The 2026 PNPL Competition: Word Classification and Efficient Cross-Subject Generalisation in LibriBrain100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03231)
*Francesco Mantegna,Gereon Elvers,Dulhan Jayalath,Gilad Landau,Tasha Kim,Miran Özdogan,Luisa Kurth,Teyun Kwon,SungJun Cho,Benjamin Ballyk,Alex Fung,Anna Greer,Pratik Somaiya,Christian Herff,Yorguin Mantilla Ramos,Hamza Abdelhedi,Karim Jerbi,Greg Farquhar,Brendan Shillingford,Mark Woolrich,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了2026年PNPL竞赛，该竞赛基于LibriBrain100数据集（含32名新被试和约80小时的单被试数据），设立Deep赛道（被试内词分类）和Broad赛道（跨被试泛化），旨在将非侵入式语音解码从靠大量单被试数据驱动，推进到仅需10-20分钟个性化数据即可实现跨用户泛化的实用脑机接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年PNPL竞赛虽然在语音检测（F1 95.6%）和音素分类（F1 73.6%）上取得显著成效，但依赖单一被试约50小时的大规模数据。实际脑机接口必须能从几分钟的数据中泛化到新用户，而非数小时。

Method: 推出LibriBrain100扩展数据集，新增32名被试（各约40分钟数据），同时将原单被试数据扩展至约80小时。竞赛设置双赛道：Deep赛道侧重被试内大规模词分类的性能极限；Broad赛道侧重跨被试泛化，并将个性化微调数据从约40分钟逐步缩减至20、10分钟。

Result: 竞赛将任务推进至词分类层面，Broad赛道将所需个性化数据压缩至10分钟这一临床上可行的时间范围，这是迈向实用非侵入性脑机接口的关键一步。

Conclusion: 通过从"数据规模驱动"转向"跨用户泛化"的课程设计，2026年PNPL竞赛代表了非侵入式语音解码从实验室演示走向临床实用的重要转折，有望为重度瘫痪患者恢复沟通能力。

Abstract: The ambition of the 2025 PNPL competition (Landau et al., 2025) was to launch a multi-year curriculum for non-invasive speech decoding. Designed to progress from foundational tasks toward the linguistic complexity required for a practical brain-computer interface (BCI), it set the stage with speech detection and phoneme classification tasks. Winning submissions reached F1-macro scores of 95.6% and 73.6% on the respective tasks (Elvers et al., 2026), highly significant advances. This success was built on the LibriBrain dataset (Özdogan et al., 2025), the largest within-subject MEG dataset recorded at the time with ${\sim}50$ hours of data for one subject. However, while within-subject scale drives strong decoding performance, a practical BCI must generalise to new users from minutes of data, not hours.
  The 2026 PNPL competition responds to this challenge with LibriBrain100 (Mantegna et al., 2026), an extended LibriBrain dataset with 32 additional subjects (${\sim}40$ minutes each) plus even more within-subject data (${\sim}80$ hours). Advancing the curriculum of tasks to focus on word classification, two complementary tracks are presented in this competition: the Deep track targets within-subject word classification at scale, aiming at the best possible performance; the Broad track targets cross-subject generalisation, progressively reducing the amount of subject-specific fine-tuning data from ${\sim}40$ to ${\sim}20$ to ${\sim}10$ minutes, a duration that falls within a clinically feasible range and brings us a step closer to a non-invasive BCI capable of restoring communication to people living with profound paralysis.

</details>
